To address these challenges, we propose the Dataset-Informed Transfer Learning (DITL) framework, which integrates dataset-derived difficulty signals with neighborhood-based triplet supervision in a unified objective. DITL introduces two adaptive components: (i) Adaptive Difficulty-Weighted Cross-Entropy (A-DWCE), which assigns per-sample weights based on k-nearest neighbor label purity in a self-supervised feature space, and (ii) Adaptive Neighborhood Representation Triplet (A-NR-Triplet), which enforces intra-class compactness and inter-class separation using a learnable margin. Unlike focal loss, DITL requires no hyperparameter tuning, removes heuristic weighting and fixed margins, and incurs negligible computational overhead, yielding a robust and scalable optimization strategy. On the large-scale VinDR-Mammo dataset, DITL achieves state-of-the-art performance for whole-image breast density classification, with significant improvements across accuracy, F1-score, and AUC (p < 0.0001). Beyond large cohorts, DITL also delivers consistent, statistically significant gains on small ROI datasets (p < 0.0001). By bridging small-scale lesion analysis with large-scale density estimation, DITL establishes a clinically relevant, scalable, and generalizable framework for mammography classification, spanning the full breast cancer screening-to-diagnosis spectrum.

</details>


### [145] [Spend Experts Where You Are Unsure: Confidence-Adaptive Routing for Mixture-of-Experts LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26052)
*Tom Saliencro,Rohan Desai,Priya Nair,Maya Lindqvist,Daniel Whitmore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE是一种基于置信度的自适应专家路由方法，通过动态调整每个token激活的专家数量，解决了传统MoE-LoRA中固定k值对简单token过度分配、对困难token分配不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE-LoRA为所有token固定分配k个专家，但不同token的模型不确定性不同：简单token的分配过于浪费，困难token的分配又不足。需要一种能根据token不确定性自适应调整专家数量的方法。

Method: CARE利用路由器输出分布作为token不确定性信号：集中分布表示高置信度，平坦分布表示模糊性。采用类似nucleus的方式激活专家，按路由器权重降序激活专家，直到累积质量达到阈值，当已激活专家意见不一致时进行小幅度扩展。通过预算调节器校准阈值，使平均激活专家数匹配目标值。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B的8个常识推理基准测试，以及数学、代码和知识任务中，CARE在相同计算量下优于固定top-k MoE-LoRA，同时以更少的激活专家数匹配固定k=4基线的性能。相同的置信度和分歧信号也优于MSP、熵和多通道代理的OOD检测。

Conclusion: CARE是一种无需额外参数、单前向传播的即插即用方法，通过自适应路由机制更高效地分配计算资源，提高了MoE-LoRA的性能和效率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) variants of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) route every token to a fixed number of experts $k$. Tokens differ in how uncertain the model is about them, so a single k over-spends on easy tokens and under-serves hard ones. We observe that the router's output distribution is already a per-token uncertainty signal: peaked mass indicates confidence, while a flat distribution indicates ambiguity. We introduce CARE (Confidence-Adaptive Routing of Experts), which admits experts in a nucleus fashion. Experts are activated in decreasing router weight until their cumulative mass reaches a threshold, with a small extension when the admitted experts disagree. A budget thermostat calibrates the threshold so that the average number of active experts matches any target. CARE is a drop-in, single-forward-pass rule with no extra parameters. Across eight commonsense benchmarks on LLaMA-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B, as well as math, code, and knowledge tasks, CARE improves over fixed top-k MoE-LoRA at matched compute and matches the fixed-k=4 baseline while activating fewer experts. The same confidence and disagreement signals also improve out-of-distribution detection over MSP, entropy, and multi-pass proxies. We support the design with nucleus fidelity, budget optimality, and an epistemic reading of disagreement, and we release code.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Egocentric Station Holding of Robotic Fish in Unknown Turbulent Background Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24860)
*Xiaozhu Lin,Xu Huang,Hongru Dai,Xiaopei Liu,Junzhi Yu,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SWiFT框架：通过强化学习为仿生机器鱼开发基于自中心反馈的定点保持策略，无需显式水流感知，在未知湍流环境中实现高效定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器鱼在自然水流环境中实现目标位置接近和定点保持是基本但未充分发展的能力，主要由于自由游动中复杂非线性流固耦合问题难以建模。

Method: 提出SWiFT框架，集成自由游动水洞实验装置、物理一致CFD模拟器和系统化的仿真到现实转移管道，通过强化学习探索自中心定点保持策略。

Result: 策略在所有指标上显著优于现有方法，特别是距离均方根误差(RMSE)表现优异；验证了仅凭自中心反馈即可在未知湍流中实现定点保持，类似于生物的趋流行为。

Conclusion: 自中心定点保持策略的成功不仅推动机器鱼控制向实际部署迈进，也表明SWiFT作为解决水下机器人复杂游泳任务的基础框架具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Approaching a target position and holding station in flowing water is a fundamental and critical capability for robotic fish operating in natural aquatic environments. Despite decades of advances in enhancing swimming efficiency and maneuverability, this capability remains underdeveloped, largely owing to the insufficiently characterized, highly nonlinear fluid-structure interactions inherent to freely swimming robotic fish in flows. To bridge this gap, we propose the SWiFT framework, a Swimming With Flow Toolbox that enables the efficient exploration of an egocentric station-holding policy for a body and/or caudal fin (BCF) robotic fish in unknown and turbulent background flows via reinforcement learning (RL). Our SWiFT integrates a free-swimming flow-tank experimental setup with a highly efficient, physically consistent computational fluid dynamics (CFD)-based simulator and a systematic sim-to-real transfer pipeline. The resulting policy achieves substantial improvements over state-of-the-art methods across all metrics, most notably root-mean-square error (RMSE) of distance. Furthermore, we validated that egocentric feedback alone, without any explicit flow sensing, enables station-holding in unknown turbulent flows, closely mirroring the biological phenomenon of rheotaxis. Accordingly, the success of this egocentric station-holding policy not only advances robotic fish control toward real-world deployment, but also highlights SWiFT's promise as a foundation for tackling complex swimming tasks for underwater robots.

</details>


### [147] [Steeringless Drifting: Differential-Torque Control of a Four-Wheel Independently Driven Vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24863)
*Sheng Zhao,Zexin Wu,Dongyang Zhou,Bolin Zhao,Xiaodong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于四轮独立驱动车辆的无转向差动转矩漂移控制方法，通过车轮转矩差产生横摆力矩实现稳定漂移


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统漂移控制依赖机械转向和后轮饱和，而转向自由的四轮独立驱动车辆可以通过差动车轮转矩产生直接横摆力矩，这为极限工况下的自动驾驶控制提供了新视角

Method: 建立了包含四轮差动驱动的双轨车辆模型，开发了漂移平衡计算方法和闭环漂移控制器

Result: 在1:10比例车辆上验证，车辆能够实现侧偏角约20°的稳定圆形漂移和八字形漂移跟踪

Conclusion: 研究证明了仅使用差动车轮转矩实现漂移控制的可行性，为转向自由车辆架构的极限控制提供了新思路

Abstract: Control methods for emerging vehicle chassis architectures are important for autonomous driving near handling limits. Unlike conventional drift control, which relies on mechanical steering and rear-tire saturation, a steering-free four-wheel independently driven (4WID) vehicle can generate direct yaw moment through differential wheel torques. This paper proposes a differential-torque drift control method for such a vehicle. A double-track vehicle model incorporating four-wheel differential actuation is established, based on which a drift-equilibrium calculation method and a closed-loop drift controller are developed. The proposed approach is validated through simulations and experiments on a 1:10-scale vehicle. The results show that the vehicle can achieve steady circular drifting with a sideslip angle of approximately 20$^\circ$ and perform figure-eight drift tracking. This study demonstrates the feasibility of drift control using only differential wheel torques and provides a new perspective on near-limit control for steering-free vehicle architectures.

</details>


### [148] [Amortising Trajectory Optimisation for Residual MPC via Implicit Contact Differentiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24959)
*Daniel Layeghi,Thomas Corbères,Calum Arnott,Aditya Kamireddypalli,Hashim Al-Obaidi,Steve Tonneau,Michael Mistry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了基于隐函数定理的微分模拟方法（IFT），用于接触丰富的轨迹优化，避免了传统有限差分和自动微分展开的高内存消耗，并结合优化器蒸馏技术提升MPC性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微分模拟方法存在成本高、内存消耗大或实现复杂的问题：有限差分法计算昂贵且对步长敏感；自动微分展开法存储不断增长的计算轨迹；KKT灵敏度推导需要复杂的手工组装。需要一种高效、内存友好的微分模拟方法。

Method: 1）基于隐函数定理（IFT）的隐式导数方法：在正则化平滑接触模型上应用AD辅助的隐式导数，通过对收敛解的驻点残差进行微分，避免求解器展开和手工组装KKT系统。2）优化器蒸馏：将批量全时域iLQR蒸馏为策略，指导短时域残差iLQR，实现残差MPC的摊销优化。

Result: 1）IFT内存效率显著提升：与展开AD相比，IFT内存随求解器迭代次数增长小于4%（vs. 10.6倍增长）；在256个接触时内存减少20倍，在16个接触和96自由度时减少6倍。2）优化器蒸馏大幅提升性能：在Finger、Franka和Unitree任务中，六步成功率比标准iLQR提高28-98个百分点。

Conclusion: IFT方法为接触丰富的轨迹优化提供了高效、内存友好的微分模拟方案，结合优化器蒸馏技术显著提升了MPC性能，在机器人控制任务中展现出优越的效率和成功率。

Abstract: Differentiable simulation can accelerate contact-rich trajectory optimisation by exposing local sensitivities of task outcomes to controls. Existing approaches either use finite differences, which are expensive and step-size sensitive; differentiate iterative contact solvers by unrolling automatic differentiation (AD), which stores a growing computation trace; or require intricate, solver-specific KKT sensitivity derivations. We introduce an AD-assisted implicit derivative for regularised smooth contacts and apply it to Mujoco MJX, based on the Implicit Function Theorem (IFT). The method differentiates the stationarity residual at the tolerance-converged solution, avoiding both solver unrolling and hand-assembled KKT systems. IFT keeps compiled temporary memory nearly constant with solver effort, changing by less than 4$\%$ from one to ten iterations versus 10.6$\times$ growth for unrolled AD. IFT memory grows slower with active contacts and model dimension, using 20$\times$ less memory at 256 contacts and 6$\times$ less at 16 contacts and 96 DoF. We further introduce optimiser distillation for residual MPC, amortising batched full-horizon iLQR into a policy that guides short-horizon residual iLQR. Across Finger, Franka, and Unitree, this raises six-step success by 28-98 percentage points over standard iLQR.

</details>


### [149] [Leveraging Semantic Maps for City-Scale Cross-View Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25215)
*Ethan Fahnestock,Erick Fuentes,Philip R Osteen,Nicholas Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种利用VLM从全景图中提取语义地标，并通过轻量级匹配器与OpenStreetMap语义地图进行跨视图匹配的机器人定位方法，在恶劣天气和夜间条件下具有良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是让机器人在未探索环境中利用OpenStreetMap等常见语义数据进行定位。现有方法要么忽略语义信息，要么过度压缩语义类别，无法充分利用丰富的语义信息。

Method: 方法包含两个关键部分：1）使用VLM从机器人全景观测中提取相关地标；2）通过蒸馏的轻量级匹配器将提取的地标与大规模语义地图中的地标进行快速关联，形成观测似然并使用贝叶斯滤波器进行时间融合。

Result: 方法在单个城市良好天气数据上训练后，能够跨地理位置、光照条件、天气状况等多种挑战进行泛化，包括在波士顿雪暴和夜间条件下收集的全景数据。发布了包含11个环境的语义提取数据集和评估轨迹。

Conclusion: 研究表明VLM能有效提取全景图中的地标并与先验语义地图建立对应关系，通过蒸馏轻量级匹配器解决了大规模地图中的计算扩展问题，实现了鲁棒的跨视图语义定位。

Abstract: We want robots to localize in previously untraversed environments against commonly available prior data. Rich semantic data available from OpenStreetMap can be useful in this task. However, existing methods either ignore this semantic information, directly matching panoramas and overhead imagery, or dramatically compress the semantic information, working with a small set of fixed classes. To leverage this rich semantic information, two challenges need to be overcome. First, useful semantic information needs to be extracted from the robot's egocentric observations. Second, the observed information must be quickly associated with the large prior semantic map (e.g., up to 628 km^2). We show that VLMs are effective at both extracting relevant landmarks from panoramas, and identifying feasible correspondences between these landmarks and prior overhead landmarks. However, using VLMs to propose all correspondences scales poorly as the number of mapped landmarks increases. Instead, we propose distilling a lightweight matcher from a VLM which computes correspondences for all entities in a map. We use this output to form an observation likelihood which is fused over time with a Bayes filter to create a time series of pose estimates. To support further investigation into generalizable cross-view methods that leverage semantic information, we release a dataset of extracted semantics and evaluation trajectories spanning eleven environments, including panoramas we collected in a snowstorm and at night in Boston. We demonstrate our method, trained on a single city's fair-weather data, generalizes across location, lighting, weather, and other challenges. Code and datasets are available at https://efahnestock.github.io/loci/.

</details>


### [150] [SGTP: Sampling-based Game-Theoretic Planning for Real-Time Multi-Vehicle Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25388)
*Zhouheng Li,Fangguo Zhao,Mattia Piccinini,Baha Zarrouki,Yuan Gao,Zitong Shan,Johannes Betz,Chen Lv,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SGTP：一种基于采样的博弈论规划框架，用于实时多车自主赛车，通过GPU加速采样和博弈感知成本函数实现多样化竞争行为，同时保证安全约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多车赛车规划器难以在策略多样性和计算效率之间取得平衡，特别是在激烈交互的实时场景中。

Method: 结合博弈论推理与GPU加速的控制序列采样和动态推演，使用博弈感知成本函数对采样轨迹排序，并强制执行轨迹边界和动态碰撞避免约束以确保可行性。

Result: 在挑战性赛道上的广泛仿真显示，SGTP在高度交互比赛中达到95.24%胜率和99.35%任务完成率，平均计算时间0.095秒，并能扩展到最多10个智能体的大规模场景。

Conclusion: SGTP成功解决了实时多车赛车规划中策略多样性与计算效率的权衡问题，为未来研究提供了开源基准和代码。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-vehicle racing requires real-time planning of diverse competitive behaviors in intense interactions. Existing planners often struggle to balance strategic diversity and computational efficiency. To address this challenge, we propose Sampling-based Game-Theoretic Planning (SGTP), a real-time framework that combines game-theoretic reasoning with GPU-accelerated sampling of control sequences and dynamics rollouts. Sampled trajectories are ranked using a game-aware cost to capture competitive interactions and generate diverse racing behaviors. Our planner then performs feasibility selection by explicitly enforcing track-boundary and dynamic collision-avoidance constraints, ensuring safe and reliable transitions between racing strategies. Extensive simulations on challenging tracks show that SGTP achieves a 95.24% win rate and a 99.35% task-completion ratio in highly interactive races, with a mean computational time of 0.095 s over multiple iterative solving steps. We also demonstrate the successful application of SGTP in large-scale scenarios with up to 10 agents. We release our code and provide an open-source benchmark of multi-agent autonomous racing algorithms to facilitate future research. Project page: https://sgtp-racing.github.io/.

</details>


### [151] [Decompose and Reorganize: Planning with Primitives and Visuomotor Policies Learned from Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25397)
*Yizhou Chen,Hang Xu,Dongjie Yu,Yupu Lu,Tengye Xu,Zeqing Zhang,Wei Zhang,Yi Ren,Ben M. Chen,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DR-LfD框架结合任务与运动规划(TAMP)和模仿学习(IL)，通过分解演示技能、建模策略约束，实现复杂机械臂操作任务的高层规划与细粒度执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TAMP在接触丰富操作中脆弱，而IL在空间泛化和多阶段操作方面能力有限。需要融合两者优势来解决复杂机械臂操作问题。

Method: 基于接触关系分解人类演示为原子技能，转换为视觉运动策略或物体中心原语，将策略的启动、终止和约束建模为TAMP兼容形式，实现不同来源技能的重组。

Result: 在真实世界和仿真环境中多场景测试显示，DR-LfD在涉及多步骤、未见设置和物理约束的任务上表现优异，将演示数据需求从指数级降低到与技能类型数量线性相关。

Conclusion: DR-LfD成功整合了TAMP的高层推理和IL的细粒度执行能力，为复杂机械臂操作提供了可扩展且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Successfully automating dexterous, long-horizon robotic manipulation requires frameworks capable of both high-level reasoning and fine-grained execution. Traditional task and motion planning (TAMP), while excellent at symbolic planning, is often brittle in contact-rich operations. Simultaneously, imitation learning (IL), while effective in manipulation tasks with visual feedback, is limited by its low capability in spatial generalization and multi-stage operation. To reconcile their complementary strengths and limitations, we propose DR-LfD (Decomposed and Reorganized Skills Learned from Demonstrations), a framework that seamlessly integrates visuomotor policies into a TAMP-gated decision-making system. Based on contact relationships, DR-LfD decomposes human demonstrations into atomic skills, which are reproduced as visuomotor policies or object-centric primitives. The initiation, termination, and constraints of the visuomotor policies are carefully modeled and implemented in a TAMP-compatible form, enabling reorganization of skills learned from different sources. DR-LfD transforms the learning problem from one requiring exponential demonstration data over possible skill sequences to one whose demonstration burden scales with the number of distinct skill types, with limited data for each skill. Through comprehensive real-world and simulation benchmarking across diverse scenarios, we demonstrate the strong performance of DR-LfD on tasks involving multiple steps, unseen setups, and physical constraints. Project website: https://dr-lfd.github.io/DR-LfD-website.

</details>


### [152] [Room-Mediated Co-occurrence for Zero-Shot Object-Centric Semantic Navigation via Frontier Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25448)
*Adam Scicluna,Gavin Paul,Alen Alempijevic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练、基于语义的零样本目标导航方法，通过房间词典调节物体关系，使用CLIP生成房间概率向量计算物体共现，通过地理传播生成价值图，在HM3D数据集上相比图像整体基线提升3%成功率和1.3%SPL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本目标导航方法直接使用潜在空间中的物体-物体相似性作为空间共现的代理，但这种关系往往较弱。需要一种更有效的方法来捕捉物体间的空间共现关系。

Method: 提出训练自由的语义导航流程：1) 将物体标签映射到CLIP衍生的房间概率向量(RPV)；2) 通过RPV分布重叠计算物体-目标共现分数；3) 使用快速行进方法进行地理洪泛传播，将分数投影到价值图上；4) 根据语义分数对边界进行排序导航。

Result: 在HM3D数据集验证集上，相比图像整体基线方法，成功率(SR)相对提升3%，加权逆路径长度(SPL)相对提升1.3%，同时保持了可解释性和开放词汇灵活性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无需训练、以物体为中心的开放词汇零样本导航流程，通过房间词典调节物体关系，改进了物体间的空间共现建模，在保持可解释性的同时提升了导航性能。

Abstract: Zero-shot ObjectNav methods increasingly use vision-language priors, but direct object-object similarity in the latent space is often a weak proxy for spatial co-occurrence. We present an analytical, training-free semantic navigation pipeline that mediates object relationships through a compact room lexicon. Each object label is mapped to a CLIP-derived Room Probability Vector (RPV), and object-target co-occurrence is computed from RPV distribution overlap. These scores are projected onto a value map using geodesic flood-fill propagation (Fast Marching Method), with adaptive signal decay, and are used to rank frontiers by semantic score for navigation. Together, these components form an integrated, training-free, object-centric pipeline for open-vocabulary zero-shot navigation. Results show that our object-centric approach improves Success Rate (SR) and Success by weighted inverse Path Length (SPL) by a relative 3% and 1.3%, respectively, compared to image-holistic baselines on the HM3D dataset validation split, while preserving interpretability and open-vocabulary flexibility. Code is available at: uts-ri.github.io/RPV-SemNav.

</details>


### [153] [A Causality-aware Infer-diagnose-refine Framework for Test-time Modality Adaptation in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25516)
*Haoyu Zhang,Yuwei Wu,Jin Chen,Gao Zhi,Zhenxin Diao,Mingyang Gao,Kun Wu,Yongchun Liu,Fan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出IDR框架，通过推断-诊断-精炼流程，在测试时动态调整视觉模态的重要性，以提升VLA模型在机器人操作任务中的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作任务中面临多模态融合的挑战，因为机器人操作涉及不同动态阶段（如长距离移动和近距离交互），视觉观察的重要性会随时间变化。现有方法难以动态调整视觉模态的重要性。

Method: 提出IDR（推断-诊断-精炼）框架，包含三个步骤：1）在事实和反事实视觉观察场景下推断动作；2）诊断视觉观察的因果效应作为动态重要性估计；3）使用训练无关的方式精炼动作预测。具体实现包括零填充干预、基于范数的量化诊断和门控残差融合。

Result: 在模拟基准和真实世界任务上的广泛实验表明，IDR框架能够提升多种VLA骨干网络的整体性能，证明了在测试时动态调整视觉重要性的有效性。

Conclusion: IDR框架是一种模型无关的测试时精炼方法，能够有效处理机器人操作中视觉重要性随时间变化的问题，通过因果推断动态调整多模态融合策略，提升VLA模型性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models predict sequential actions to execute tasks specified by language instructions, conditioned on visual observations and proprioceptive states. However, how to fuse modalities in VLA models remains an open problem, since robot manipulation involves dynamic phases, such as long-distance movements and close-range interactions, in which the importance of visual observations may vary over time. In this paper, we propose an infer-diagnose-refine (IDR) framework, a model-agnostic framework that can be integrated with diverse VLA architectures for refining action predictions at test time. IDR first infers actions under factual and counterfactual scenarios of visual observations, and then diagnoses the causal effects of visual observations as the estimated dynamic importance, which is finally used to refine the action predictions in a training-free manner. We further design a causality-aware action refiner to realize the IDR framework, including zero-padding interventions for inferring counterfactual actions, norm-based quantification for diagnosing causal effects, and gated residual fusion for refining actions. Extensive experiments on both simulation benchmarks and real-world tasks show improvements in overall performance across multiple VLA backbones, demonstrating the efficacy of dynamically adjusting visual importance at test time.

</details>


### [154] [P3: Probabilistic Policy Propagation for Stable VAE-Based Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25541)
*Liyun Yan,Jianming Ma,Yang Zhang,Shengcheng Fu,Zhanxiang Cao,Keqi Zhu,Yizhi Chen,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: P^3 (Probabilistic Policy Propagation) 是一个用于VAE基础策略的分布感知优化框架，解决了VAE的随机潜在空间与PPO算法之间的不匹配问题，显著提升了数据效率和收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分自编码器(VAE)在机器人学中广泛用于编码高维噪声观测，但其随机潜在空间与PPO算法存在不匹配：有效策略需要对潜在分布进行边缘化，而先前实现仅使用单个潜在样本来估计概率比和KL散度，导致显著的方差和偏差。

Method: 提出P^3 (Probabilistic Policy Propagation)框架，将基于矩的概率方法与基于采样的校准相结合。该方法在随机潜在空间中进行分布感知优化，实现稳定高效的学习和鲁棒策略行为。

Result: P^3将数据效率从64.6%提升到>96%，减少收敛步数>20%。在挑战性的人形跑酷任务中表现出色，为VAE基础的PPO提供了有效基础。

Conclusion: P^3解决了VAE随机潜在空间与PPO之间的理论不匹配问题，通过分布感知优化显著提升了学习效率和策略性能，为基于VAE的强化学习提供了可靠框架。

Abstract: Variational Autoencoders are widely used to encode high-dimensional and noisy observations in robotics. However, their stochastic latent creates a mismatch with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO): an effective policy marginalizes over the latent distribution, whereas former implementations estimate its probability ratio and KL divergence using only one latent sample. We identify a fundamental but overlooked theoretical cause: naive single-sample approximations in stochastic latent space induce significant variance and bias in the surrogate loss. To address this, we introduce P^3 (Probabilistic Policy Propagation), a distribution-aware optimization framework for VAE-based policies. $P^3$ couples moment-based probabilistic method for stable and efficient learning with sampling-based calibration for robust policy behavior under latent uncertainty. In our experiments, P^3 boosts data efficiency from 64.6% to >96%, reduces convergence steps by >20%. Furthermore, P^3 is evaluated on challenging humanoid parkour tasks and shows an effective foundation for VAE-based PPO. Code is available at https://github.com/ylyem9x/P3_Open.

</details>


### [155] [When Does Legacy Data Start to Help? Emergent Transfer in Cross-Configuration Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25593)
*Tao Wang,Hudson Hou,Yingdong Hu,Yufeng Liu,Qinghai Li,Yingjie Jiang,Yingzhi Wang,Cheng Ma,Richard Wang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究机器人硬件升级后，旧配置的演示数据何时能有效帮助新配置学习任务，发现存在"顿悟式"三阶段模式：低能力时无效，达到阈值后收益激增，高能力时收益递减


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人硬件会随时间升级，但演示数据通常与特定传感器和执行器配置绑定。实际中未充分探索的问题是：旧硬件数据何时开始对升级后的机器人有益？研究旨在回答这一实用性问题。

Method: 在轮式人形机器人平台上进行两代硬件实验，相机和夹具改变但整体形态保持不变。通过跨配置数据协同训练，观察不同任务能力阶段的数据利用效果。提供基于梯度对齐和剩余策略不确定性的理论解释。

Result: 观察到三阶段模式：1)低能力时(10.0%→10.0%)旧数据无益；2)跨越阈值后收益激增(23.3%→86.7%，花朵插入任务)；3)高能力时收益递减(85.0%→93.3%，笔插入任务)。在移动双臂浇水任务中得到验证。

Conclusion: 旧硬件数据并非总是有益，其效果取决于新配置的任务能力水平。研究提出了基于阶段的决策规则：何时收集新硬件数据、何时重用旧演示。这对机器人硬件升级时的数据利用策略具有实际指导意义。

Abstract: Robotic hardware evolves over time, but demonstration data is often tied to a specific sensor and actuator configuration. This raises a practical and underexplored question: when does legacy data begin to benefit an upgraded robot? We study this question on a wheeled humanoid platform across two hardware generations, where both the camera and gripper are changed while the overall morphology remains fixed. Contrary to the common assumption that more cross-configuration data is always helpful, we observe a grokking-like transition: legacy data remains ineffective until the upgraded configuration acquires a minimum level of task competence, after which co-training gains rise sharply before diminishing near saturation. We hypothesize that this task-dependent transition is governed by a transfer threshold and characterize the resulting three-phase pattern. Across real-robot manipulation tasks, we observe all three phases: no measurable benefit at low competence ($10.0\% \rightarrow 10.0\%$), a sharp gain after crossing the threshold ($23.3\% \rightarrow 86.7\%$ on flower insertion), and diminishing returns at high competence ($85.0\% \rightarrow 93.3\%$ on pen insertion). We provide a theoretical account based on gradient alignment and residual policy uncertainty, and derive a phase-aware rule for deciding when to collect more new-hardware data and when to reuse legacy demonstrations. We further validate this three-phase pattern on a mobile dual-arm watering task, with results consistent with our predictions.

</details>


### [156] [Tri-Manual Visuomotor Imitation Learning of Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25731)
*James Zhao,Mingyuan Ba,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TriManPolicy是一个三臂模仿学习系统，通过依赖感知三臂调度（DATS）算法重新安排三臂演示的时间，使单操作员能够为三臂系统提供演示，从而训练出同步协调的三臂策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三臂系统的遥操作存在挑战：机器人可以同时协调三个手臂，但单个操作员只能连续控制两个。传统的成对模式切换会导致将原本独立的动作按顺序记录，使得行为克隆复制的是界面延迟而非任务所需的时间安排。

Method: 提出TriManPolicy系统，核心是依赖感知三臂调度（DATS）。DATS离线重新安排演示时间，保留固定持续时间的局部传感器运动段，并根据任务顺序和手臂使用的约束重新定位这些段，这些约束由人工审核。最终训练一个同步的三臂策略。

Result: 在六个真实世界挑战性任务中，使用DATS重新安排时间后的演示训练的策略表现出更高效的协调性，同时保持可比较的观察任务成功率。离线分析进一步表明DATS改变了跨手臂的监督，而不仅仅是移除空闲期。

Conclusion: TriManPolicy通过DATS算法有效解决了三臂遥操作中操作员控制通道不足的问题，使单操作员能够为三臂系统提供演示，并训练出同步协调的三臂策略，在实际任务中表现出更好的协调效率。

Abstract: Bimanual teleoperation provides an effective way to collect robot demonstrations, but it assumes that the operator and robot have matching numbers of simultaneous control channels. This assumption breaks for tri-manual systems: the robot can coordinate three arms concurrently, whereas a single operator can continuously control only two. Pairwise mode switching may therefore record otherwise independent motions sequentially, causing behaviour cloning to reproduce delays imposed by the interface rather than required by the task. We present TriManPolicy, a tri-manual imitation learning system that allows one operator to demonstrate behaviours for three arms. Its central component is Dependency-Aware Tri-Arm Scheduling (DATS). The key idea is to preserve the demonstrated arm motions while reconsidering when they occur. DATS retimes demonstrations offline by preserving local sensorimotor segments of fixed duration and repositioning them according to constraints on task order and arm usage that are reviewed by a human. The resulting data train a single synchronous policy for all three arms, while deployment requires neither the dependency graph nor the scheduler. Across six challenging tasks performed in the real world, policies trained on demonstrations retimed by DATS exhibit more efficient coordination while maintaining comparable observed task success. Offline analysis further shows that DATS changes the supervision across arms rather than merely removing idle periods.

</details>


### [157] [Cooperative Multi-UAV Navigation in Complex Environments via Systematic Multi-Agent Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25754)
*Yu Su,Nabil Aouf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个多智能体深度强化学习框架，通过协调探索、示范利用、安全课程调度和结构感知泛化，解决无人机复杂环境中导航的局部最优陷阱、稀疏奖励、智能体学习不平衡和跨场景泛化不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体无人机在复杂环境中的协同导航面临局部最优陷阱、稀疏奖励、智能体间学习不平衡、跨场景泛化不足等关键挑战，需要系统性解决方案。

Method: 1) 结合访问状态记忆、方向性新颖性估计和惩罚反向传播的感知机制；2) 基于团队协作程度分层的协作示范缓冲区；3) 安全感知的双条件课程调度机制；4) 将局部几何特征抽象为领域参数的结构感知门控网络和专家混合机制。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该框架在协作成功率、导航鲁棒性、零样本跨场景泛化能力和动态环境适应性方面表现优异，并在混合静态-动态障碍物设置下验证了鲁棒适应性。

Conclusion: 提出的多智能体深度强化学习框架有效解决了复杂环境中无人机协同导航的关键挑战，实现了高性能、安全、可泛化的协同导航系统。

Abstract: Cooperative navigation of multi-agent UAVs in complex environments faces key challenges including local optima traps, sparse rewards, learning imbalance among agents, and insufficient cross-scenario generalisation. This paper proposes a multi-agent deep reinforcement learning framework that addresses these issues through coordinated exploration, demonstration exploitation, safe curriculum scheduling, and structure-aware generalisation. First, a perception mechanism combining memory of visited states, directional novelty estimates, and penalty backpropagation enables agents to proactively detect and escape local optima. Second, a hierarchical collaborative demonstration buffer with tiered behaviour cloning manages trajectories by degree of team collaboration and applies differential supervision to the actor network, improving demonstration utilisation under sparse collaborative signals. Third, a safety-aware dual-condition curriculum scheduling mechanism reviews mastered scenarios through back-testing and experience pre-filling during training, suppressing catastrophic forgetting while ensuring both task performance and flight safety. For generalisation, local geometric features computed from sensor readings are abstracted into a domain parameter, through which a structure-aware gating network and mixture-of-experts mechanism condition the policy on local structural patterns rather than scenario-specific coordinates, enabling cross-scenario transfer without exposure to the target environment. The framework is further validated under mixed static-dynamic obstacle settings, showing robust adaptability to dynamic disturbances. Simulation results confirm strong performance in collaboration success rate, navigation robustness, zero-shot cross-scenario generalisation, and dynamic environment adaptability.

</details>


### [158] [Tripody: An Overconstrained 3-SPR-like Parallel Robot for High-Reach Construction Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25781)
*Julien Kindle,Jakub Raczy,Riccardo Balbi,Andrea Alessandretti,Cesar Cadena,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Tripody是一种用于天花板施工的轮式3自由度并联机器人，采用过约束的3-SPR结构，通过弹性变形吸收运动不兼容性，实现高刚度、毫米级精度的天花板作业。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统天花板施工依赖笨重的串行机械臂，难以在杂乱室内环境中部署，需要轻量级、高可部署性的替代方案，能够在达到天花板高度的同时保持毫米级精度和足够的刚度来支撑工具负载。

Method: 将经典的3-SPR并联机构（3条支链；S：基座球铰；P：驱动棱柱关节；R：末端旋转关节）的基座球铰替换为万向节，故意过约束机构；通过小范围分布式弹性变形吸收由此产生的不兼容性，保持主要为平移运动。系统包括定制线性执行器、运动学兼容性分析，以及结合SE(3)状态估计、正向运动学和任务空间控制的控制架构。

Result: 33kg系统高度从1.7m扩展到3.4m，支持连续32kg负载。相比球铰基座变体，具有相似的面内刚度但显著更高的扭转刚度：在1.7m处增加67%，2.6m处增加196%，3.4m处增加454%。闭环定位在全工作空间内收敛到0.6mm以下；纯模型外推的95%误差为2.7mm（最大3.6mm）。开环天花板钻孔实验中，钻孔15孔模式的最大相对孔位误差为4.5mm。

Conclusion: 过约束、通过柔顺性吸收运动不兼容性的3-SPR结构是构建轻量级、高可达、毫米级精度施工机器人的实用途径。

Abstract: Many ceiling construction tasks still rely on heavy serial manipulators that are difficult to deploy in cluttered interiors, motivating lightweight, field-ready alternatives that reach ceiling height while maintaining millimeter-level accuracy and the stiffness demanded by overhead tool loads. We introduce Tripody, a wheeled 3-DoF parallel robot for high-reach tasks that replaces the base spherical joints of a classical 3-SPR (3 legs; S: base spherical joint; P: actuated prismatic joint; R: end-effector revolute joint) morphology with universal joints, intentionally overconstraining the mechanism; small, distributed elastic deflections absorb the resulting incompatibilities, preserving predominantly translational motion. The 33kg system extends from 1.7m to 3.4m in height, supports a continuous 32kg payload, and offers a modular end-effector interface for ceiling operations. We detail the mechanical design - including custom linear actuators and a kinematic-compatibility analysis - and a control stack for accurate positioning that combines SE(3) state estimation, forward kinematics, and task-space control. In experiments, Tripody exhibits similar in-plane stiffness to a spherical-base variant but substantially higher torsional stiffness - an increase of 67% at 1.7m, 196% at 2.6m, and 454% at 3.4m - while maintaining negligible cross-axis coupling. Closed-loop positioning with a total station converges below 0.6mm across the entire workspace; pure model extrapolation achieves a 95th-percentile error of 2.7mm (max 3.6mm). Finally, we demonstrate task-level ceiling-drilling feasibility in an open-loop study by drilling a 15-hole pattern with 4.5mm maximum relative hole-position error after rigid alignment. These results support overconstrained, compliance-absorbing 3-SPR-like architectures as a practical path to lightweight, high- reach, millimeter-accurate construction robots.

</details>


### [159] [Motion-Acceleration Calibration and Compensation in IMUs without External Equipment for Attitude Estimation Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25784)
*Fabian Arzberger,Andreas Nüchter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种利用陀螺仪测量补偿系统运动引起的附加加速度（离心加速度和切向加速度）的方法，同时引入了一种无需外部设备的IMU内参（轴偏差、偏置、尺度）和外参（基座到IMU向量）校准方法，显著提升了依赖重力方向的姿态估计滤波器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动系统运动时，惯性测量单元（IMU）会受到附加加速度的影响，这会扭曲局部重力的测量。这种影响在IMU距离系统基座越远时越严重。现有姿态估计滤波器（如Madgwick、Mahony）在高速运动时依赖陀螺仪积分，但这种方法容易在重力矢量周围积累长期误差。

Method: 利用陀螺仪测量来补偿系统运动引起的附加加速度（离心加速度和切向加速度）。同时提出了一种校准方法，可以估计IMU的内参（轴偏差、偏置、尺度）和外参（基座到IMU向量），无需额外外部设备。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界中的评估表明，该方法显著改善了任何依赖重力方向的姿态滤波器。在高度动态系统和无法将IMU置于旋转中心的系统（如球形移动测绘系统）上展示了有效性。

Conclusion: 通过陀螺仪补偿附加加速度并结合IMU参数校准，可以有效提升移动系统在动态运动中的姿态估计精度，特别是在IMU远离旋转中心的情况下。

Abstract: Attitude estimation based on inertial sensing requires measurements of local angular velocities and local gravity via gyroscopes and accelerometers.
  However, during the motion of a mobile system the inertial measurement unit (IMU) will be subject to additional accelerations which skews the measurement of local gravity.
  This effect gets amplified the further away the IMU is from the base of the system.
  Many attitude estimation filters, such as "Madgwick" or "Mahony", account for this by relying more on gyroscope integration for periods of high angular velocity.
  However, this approach is prone to accumulate long term error especially around the gravity vector.
  In this work we utilize the gyroscope measurements to compensate the additional accelerations induced by the motion of the system, i.e., centripetal- and tangential-accelerations.
  Additionally, we introduce a calibration method that estimates intrinsic IMU parameters such as axes misalignment, bias, scale, as well as the extrinsic base-to-IMU vector without the necessity for additional external equipment.
  Our evaluation in simulation as well as in the real-world shows that this method improves any attitude filter that relies on the direction of gravity.
  Furthermore we demonstrate the effectivenes on highly dynamic systems, and systems that are unable to put the IMU at the center of rotation, using our real-world spherical mobile mapping system.

</details>


### [160] [Transformer Transformer: A Unified Model for Motion-Conditioned Robot Co-design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25798)
*Huy Ha,C. Karen Liu,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Transformer Transformer是一种基于扩散变换器的机器人协同设计框架，通过统一标记化技术处理机器人形态、状态和动作，能够零样本优化未见过的奖励函数和轨迹，显著提升机器人性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操纵性能常被忽视的一个因素是机器人本身的形态设计。本研究旨在通过运动条件化的机器人协同设计，生成能够跟踪目标末端执行器轨迹（来自人类演示）同时优化用户定义奖励的完整机器人设计。

Method: 提出Transformer Transformer，这是一个在RoboTokens（机器人形态、状态和动作的统一标记化）上训练的扩散变换器。该架构适用于各种形态空间（如轮式双臂、四足、人形机器人）和用例（形态生成、跨形态控制器）。通过动力学自引导程序，将奖励无关的状态和动作预测转换为奖励特定的价值预测，从而引导形态扩散向高价值机器人设计。

Result: 在多个设计空间中的实验显示，该方法能够零样本优化未见过的奖励和轨迹，相比进化基线提高了性能并减少了运行时间。最终制造了一个优化的ALOHA设计，相比原始设计将跟踪误差降低了70%以上。

Conclusion: Transformer Transformer框架展示了在机器人协同设计中，通过统一标记化和动力学自引导，能够有效生成优化的机器人形态设计，显著提升运动跟踪性能，为机器人形态与控制的协同优化提供了新方法。

Abstract: An often overlooked factor of robot manipulation performance is the embodiment of the robot itself. Motivated by this problem, we study motion-conditioned robot co-design, where the goal is to generate complete robot designs that track target end-effector trajectories (from human demonstrations) while optimizing user-defined rewards. We introduce Transformer Transformer, a diffusion transformer trained on RoboTokens, a unified tokenization of robot embodiments, states, and actions. The same architecture can be used across embodiment spaces (e.g., wheeled bimanual, quadrupeds, humanoids) and use cases (embodiment generation, cross embodiment controller). Rather than overfitting to one reward function, Transformer Transformer is a dynamics model, whose reward-agnostic state and action predictions can be converted into reward-specific value predictions. These value predictions are used to steer embodiment diffusion towards high value robot designs, through a procedure we call Dynamics Self-Guidance. Experiments across multiple design spaces show zero-shot optimization of unseen rewards and trajectories, improving performance and runtime over the evolutionary baseline. Finally, we fabricated an optimized ALOHA design, which reduced tracking error by over 70% compared to the original design.

</details>


### [161] [Modular Robotic Catheters for Endovascular Aneurysm Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25807)
*Alex Ranne,Jinshi Zhao,Ali Anil Demircali,Songli Moey,Ayhan Aktas,Burak Temelkuran,Nassir Navab,Ferdinando Rodriguez y Baena*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一种用于复杂主动脉瘤腔内修复术的2段式可转向导管系统，通过模块化腱驱动平台增强操作灵活性，旨在缩短手术时间并解决血管通路挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统商业器械在分支型主动脉瘤腔内修复术中难以有效进入血管分支，导致手术时间延长和并发症增加，需要更灵巧的导管系统来解决这一临床挑战。

Method: 1) 设计具有4自由度的2段式可转向导管；2) 开发模块化腱驱动平台以应对系统冗余；3) 采用热纤维拉制技术规模化制造导管，并通过激光微加工软化导管尖端；4) 通过模拟和体外实验评估系统性能。

Result: 通过模拟评估了导管的弯曲刚度，在血管模型中进行了转向性能的体外实验验证，证明该系统具有增强操作灵活性的潜力。

Conclusion: 这种手持式机器人可转向导管系统有望缩短未来腔内手术时间，为临床医生处理复杂病例提供有效工具。

Abstract: Fenestrated/Branched endovascular aneurysm repair (FEVAR/BEVAR) require surgeons to navigate catheters and guidewires into various branches of the abdominal aorta, before deploying stent grafts to alleviate pressure on the aneurysm. Previous clinical studies suggests that surgeons continue to struggle with vessel access using standard commercial instruments, prolonging the procedural time and inducing further complications. In this work, we present two contributions to solving this problem: 1) A bespoke 2-segment steerable catheter, consisting of 4 degrees of freedom to enhance dexterity. 2) An expandable, modular tendon-driven actuation platform that can accommodate for the redundancies introduced in our system. To fabricate the catheter, we capitalized on thermal fiber drawing, a technique that creates high-aspect ratio devices at scale, and processed the catheter with laser micro-machining to soften its tip. We evaluated the system using simulations, where we investigated the catheter's bending stiffness, then its steerability with in-vitro experiments in vascular phantoms. This handheld, robotic steerable catheter system has the potential to shorten the length of future endovascular surgeries, and give clinicians the tools to resolve challenging clinical cases.

</details>


### [162] [HiFi-UMI: Learning Deployable Manipulation Policies from High-Fidelity UMI Data Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25895)
*Simple AI,:,Yuteng Wei,Jinming Ma,Jiawei Wang,Weitao Zhou,Yushen Zuo,Ke Rui,Minglei Li,Jinhao Zhang,Zhikang Pan,Xiang Wang,Haoran Jia,Huan Du,Zicheng Zeng,Jun Ma,Guiyu Qin,Di Zhang,Xiaofei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiFi-UMI系统通过提高机器人无数据采集的保真度，实现了零机器人后训练，使仅用高质量UMI数据训练的模型可直接部署到真实机器人上，性能接近甚至超过真实机器人数据训练的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人学习面临高质量数据稀缺的瓶颈。真实机器人遥操作数据准确但成本高，而机器人无数据采集（UMI）虽然可扩展，但通常只能用于预训练，仍需少量真实机器人数据进行后训练。研究探索能否通过提高UMI数据的保真度来完全替代真实机器人数据。

Method: 提出HiFi-UMI系统，通过头戴式离线立体惯性SLAM、原生相对位姿估计、微秒级同步触发和每手两个广角相机（覆盖约200度视野）等技术，实现高精度轨迹采集。系统达到3毫米末端执行器精度，无需外部跟踪基础设施。

Result: 仅用HiFi-UMI数据进行后训练的模型可直接部署到真实机器人上，性能与真实机器人遥操作基线相当。在三个不同骨干模型上，成功率差异分别为-2.5、+3.1和-0.6个百分点。最强模型在精确插入任务上达到85%成功率。预训练4000小时数据可将未见任务的行动误差降低41%，并将真实机器人成功率提高18.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过提高UMI数据的保真度，可以完全替代真实机器人数据进行后训练，为机器人学习社区提供了大规模、高保真的数据资源。开源了HiFi-UMI-2K数据集，包含2000小时微秒同步、超广视野的演示数据。

Abstract: Learning deployable manipulation policies is bottlenecked by the scarcity of data that is both high-fidelity and scalable. Real-robot teleoperation is accurate but costly to scale; robot-free UMI capture scales readily, and current practice uses the resulting data mainly for pre-training, adding a small real-robot "anchor" at post-training. We ask whether raising the fidelity of robot-free UMI data, rather than shrinking the real-robot fraction, can remove that anchor. We present HiFi-UMI, a portable UMI data-production system co-designed for trajectory accuracy, inter-gripper relative pose, synchronization, and field of view: head-mounted offline stereo-inertial SLAM, native rather than reconstructed relative pose, a shared microsecond GPIO trigger, and two wide-angle cameras per hand covering ~200 degrees. It reaches 3 mm workspace-local end-effector accuracy without external tracking infrastructure. Using this corpus, we demonstrate zero-robot post-training: a policy post-trained solely on HiFi-UMI demonstrations deploys directly on a real robot and matches in-domain teleoperation across three backbones spanning the vision-language-action and world-action-model families, with success-rate differences of -2.5, +3.1, and -0.6 percentage points on StarVLA-QwenPI, OpenPI-pi_0.5, and LingBot-VA; the strongest policy reaches 85% on a precision insertion task, even though the teleoperation baseline is collected in the evaluation scene and no HiFi-UMI trajectory is. Pre-training on 4,000 hours from the same corpus lowers action error on ten unseen tasks by 41% and, on StarVLA-QwenPI, raises real-robot success by a further 18.1 percentage points. We open-source HiFi-UMI-2K, 2,000 hours of microsecond-synchronized, ultra-wide-FoV demonstrations, each automatically reconstructed and validated through simulation replay, as a large-scale, high-fidelity resource for the robot-learning community.

</details>


### [163] [SAM3D-Guided Object-Centric Representation Alignment for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25912)
*Zonghe Liu,Shanyuan Jie,Xiaoquan Sun,Chen Cao,Zetian Xu,Zongsheng Liu,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于π₀的物体中心3D表示对齐框架，使用SAM3D作为冻结的3D教师，在训练时提供目标物体3D先验，提升VLA模型在遮挡、姿态变化等复杂场景下的3D理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型大多依赖2D视觉语言骨干，缺乏对目标物体的细粒度3D理解，特别是在遮挡、姿态变化、尺度变化和精确空间交互等复杂场景下表现不足。

Method: 使用物体识别模型定位任务相关物体并生成掩码，通过SAM3D提取密集的物体级3D表示，与π₀的中间视觉特征对齐，使策略能够内化目标物体3D信息。

Result: 在仿真实验中，LIBERO任务达到99.1%成功率，CALVIN任务平均长度为4.11。真实世界实验证明该方法在需要关注不同目标物体的长时程操作场景中特别有效。

Conclusion: 提出的物体中心3D表示对齐框架能够有效提升VLA模型对目标物体的3D理解，同时保持原有的RGB-语言到动作推理流程，无需在测试时使用深度、点云等额外3D模块。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for general robot manipulation, but most existing models rely on 2D visual-language backbones and lack fine-grained 3D understanding of target objects, especially under occlusion, pose variation, scale changes, and precise spatial interaction. We propose an object-centric 3D representation alignment framework built upon $π_0$, using SAM3D as a frozen 3D teacher to provide target-object 3D priors during training. Specifically, we localize task-relevant objects with object recognition models, generate corresponding object masks, and use SAM3D to extract dense object-level 3D representations, which are aligned with intermediate visual features of $π_0$. This enables the policy to internalize target-object 3D information while preserving the original RGB-language-to-action inference pipeline without requiring depth, point clouds, masks, SAM3D, or additional 3D modules at test time. Simulation experiments show consistent improvements, achieving 99.1\% on LIBERO and an average length of 4.11 on CALVIN. Real-world experiments further demonstrate that our method is particularly effective in long-horizon manipulation scenarios where the robot must focus on different target objects across multiple subtasks.

</details>


### [164] [DC-WAM: Dynamic-Centric Visual Supervision and Reasoning for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25918)
*Haoyuan Ji,Lingxiang Fan,Shang Su,Yinqiao Lu,Mengkai Shi,Jun Gao,Shuo Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DC-WAM是一个动态中心的世界行动模型，通过重新分配RGB视频分支的监督和计算，强调交互诱导的视觉动态而非外观重建，从而提升策略性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统WAMs使用光真实感未来预测，但计算量大且可能过度关注与动作选择弱相关的纹理、光照和背景变化。研究发现视频分支的主要价值可能在于训练中诱导出的控制相关视觉表示，而非渲染的未来本身。

Method: 提出DC-WAM框架：在监督层面，结合时间差分流匹配和轨迹引导加权，强调密集时间变化和抓取器、操作对象、接触区域的局部运动区域；在推理层面，DynaRoute预测token级动态相关性并转换为注意力偏置，引导模型关注控制相关的未来token。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界操作任务中的实验表明，DC-WAM能持续提升策略性能，特别是在光照、物体外观和背景纹理等分布外扰动下表现更优。

Conclusion: 通过从外观主导的重建转向交互诱导的视觉动态，DC-WAM在不增加额外模态特定预测或在线输入的情况下，有效提升了WAMs的控制性能。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) augment robot policies with future visual prediction, but it remains unclear what the visual modality should learn for control. While photorealistic future prediction provides dense supervision, it also incurs substantial computation and can allocate capacity to texture, illumination, and background variations that are only weakly related to action selection. Recent efficient WAM variants suggest that the main benefit of the video branch may not lie in the rendered future itself, but in the control-relevant visual representations induced during training. In this work, we revisit future video prediction from a dynamic-centric perspective and ask whether an existing RGB-based WAM can be redirected from appearance-dominated reconstruction toward interaction-induced visual dynamics without introducing additional modality-specific predictions or online inputs at deployment. We propose DC-WAM, a dynamic-centric WAM framework that redistributes supervision and computation in the RGB video branch. At the supervision level, DC-WAM combines temporal-difference flow matching with trajectory-guided weighting, emphasizing dense temporal changes and localized regions where the gripper, manipulated objects, and contact areas move. At the reasoning level, DynaRoute predicts token-wise dynamic relevance and converts it into an attention bias, guiding the model toward control-relevant future tokens. Experiments in simulation and on real-world manipulation tasks show that DC-WAM consistently improves policy performance, especially under out-of-distribution perturbations in lighting, object appearance, and background texture.

</details>


### [165] [Physics-Aware End-to-End Deep Reinforcement Learning for Quadcopter Control with Actuator Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25985)
*Ya-Chia Shen,Woei-Leong Chan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究了面向无人机（特别是四旋翼）的物理感知端到端深度强化学习控制方法，直接作用于低层机体输入（总推力和机体扭矩），通过高保真Simulink环境实现闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机（尤其是四旋翼）因其欠驱动特性（仅有4个控制输入控制6个自由度）给自主控制带来独特挑战，需要研究能直接作用于低层控制输入的端到端深度强化学习方法。

Method: 采用物理感知的端到端深度强化学习方法，直接控制总推力和机体扭矩；构建高保真Simulink仿真环境，包含12状态刚体模型、基于Moore-Penrose伪逆的Action2RPM分配、带时间常数的一阶电机动态模型和转子陀螺耦合；设计平衡目标到达和稳定性的奖励函数；评估DDPG、TD3、PPO、SAC四种DRL算法。

Result: SAC和TD3在稳定性和探索效率方面表现最佳，PPO的样本效率较低；研究强调了建模执行器延迟和气动矩对稳定低层控制的重要性，为四旋翼DRL提供了可复现的基准。

Conclusion: 物理感知的端到端深度强化学习能有效解决四旋翼欠驱动控制问题，SAC和TD3算法表现优异，精确的执行器动态和气动效应建模对稳定低层控制至关重要，研究为四旋翼DRL提供了基准平台。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), particularly quadcopters, present unique challenges for autonomous control due to their underactuated dynamics: only four available control inputs must govern six degrees of freedom. This paper investigates a physics-aware, end-to-end deep reinforcement learning (DRL) approach that acts directly on low-level body inputs, total thrust and body torques $(T, τ_x, τ_y, τ_z)$, and closes the loop through a high-fidelity Simulink environment. Our simulator integrates a 12-state rigid-body model (MATLAB Level-2 S-Function) with (i) an Action2RPM allocation based on the Moore-Penrose pseudo-inverse of a coefficient matrix derived from thrust and drag terms, and (ii) first-order actuator dynamics for each motor (time constant $T_m = 0.076$ s), including rotor gyroscopic coupling. A shaped reward balances goal-reaching and stability using an exponential position well, attitude penalties, and quadratic velocity costs. Four DRL algorithms, DDPG, TD3, PPO, and SAC, are evaluated in two stages: (S1) thrust-only hover and (S2) hover with pitch torque and a translated goal. Results show that SAC and TD3 achieve superior stability and exploration efficiency, while PPO is less sample-efficient. The study highlights the significance of modeling actuator lags and aerodynamic moments for stable low-level control and provides a reproducible benchmark for quadcopter DRL.

</details>


### [166] [S2A2: Audio-Visual Imitation Learning for Manipulation Tasks Using Acoustic Spatial Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26047)
*Kaneyoshi Hiratsuka,Benjamin Yen,Ryosuke Kojima*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于听觉感知的机器人操作任务和S2A2多模态模仿学习框架，通过整合视觉、声学空间和声学信号信息，让机器人利用听觉线索确定操作目标


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声学信息能提供物体位置、材料特性和接触或运动引起变化的丰富线索，但现有机器人操作任务主要依赖视觉，忽视了听觉信息的重要作用

Method: 提出了S2A2多模态模仿学习框架，整合视觉特征、声学空间信息和声学信号信息，并实现了基于ACT、Diffusion Policy、VQ-BeT和π_0等策略的模型

Result: 仿真实验表明该方法在对位置和音色都有要求的任务中最有效；真实机器人实验证实了所提任务和框架在现实世界操作中的适用性

Conclusion: 声学感知操作任务和S2A2框架为机器人提供了利用听觉线索进行主动探索的能力，扩展了多模态感知在机器人操作中的应用

Abstract: Acoustic information provides rich cues about object location, material properties, and changes caused by contact or motion. This paper introduces a new set of acoustic-aware manipulation tasks for imitation learning, in which robots must use auditory cues to determine manipulation targets. These tasks require sound source localization and identification for active exploration in robotic manipulation. Also, we propose a multimodal imitation learning framework, Spatial-Spectral Audio Action (S2A2), that integrates visual features with acoustic spatial and acoustic signal information for the acoustic-aware manipulation tasks. We implemented S2A2 models that integrates policies such as ACT, Diffusion Policy, VQ-BeT, and $π_0$, into our framework. Simulation experiments showed that the proposed method is the most effective for tasks requiring both position and timbre. Furthermore, real-robot experiments confirm the applicability of the proposed tasks and framework to real-world manipulation.

</details>


### [167] [$π\mathbf{R}^2$: Reactive Real-time Flow Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26055)
*Sungjae Park,Shubham Tulsiani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: πR²是一种改进动作分块流策略的方法，通过分离快速和慢速感知通道，以及延迟自适应调度，使大模型策略能够在保持多模态表达能力的同时实现实时反应性控制，显著提升动态环境中的操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于大预训练模型的动作分块流策略在运行时采用开环执行，无法在动作执行过程中响应感知输入，缺乏反应性。虽然更频繁的重规划可以恢复反应性，但感知-动作管道的延迟太大（大骨干网络+多步去噪过程），无法实现高频重规划，导致动作过时，不适合动态闭环控制。

Method: πR²基于扩散强制的位置噪声调度，提出两个核心创新：1）将条件输入分为快速通道（本体感知，每刻更新）和异步更新的慢速通道（视觉语言特征），使策略能在分块内响应本体感知同时容忍过时的视觉信息；2）延迟自适应流调度将飞行中的动作作为修复条件，每次调用只执行一步去噪，让训练好的模型能适应不同硬件延迟。该方法对现有架构改动最小，可从预训练策略微调得到。

Result: 在真实xArm6+XHand平台上基于GR00T-N1.7实现，πR²的闭环重规划速度比基础策略快约4倍（A5000 GPU上约25Hz），每40毫秒基于新鲜观察执行动作。在仿真和真实世界操作任务中，πR²相比最强基线在仿真中成功率提升高达23%，在真实世界中提升30%。

Conclusion: πR²成功解决了大模型动作分块策略的反应性和延迟问题，使这些策略能够在保持大骨干网络、多模态表达能力和多动作预测优势的同时，实现实时闭环控制，显著提升了动态环境中的操作性能。

Abstract: Generalist manipulation policies increasingly take the form of action-chunking flow policies built on large pretrained backbones. Such chunks run open-loop, so the policy cannot react to sensory input arriving mid-execution, sacrificing \emph{reactivity}. Replanning more often would restore it, but the perception-to-action pipeline (a large backbone plus multiple denoising steps) is too slow: this \emph{latency} forbids frequent replanning and leaves committed actions stale, making such policies ill-suited for dynamic, closed-loop control. We present $π\mathbf{R}^2$, which makes these policies reactive and real-time while retaining large backbones, expressive multi-modal policies, and multi-action prediction. Built on the per-position noise schedule of diffusion forcing, $π\mathbf{R}^2$ contributes two ideas. First, it splits conditioning into a fast channel (proprioception, fresh every tick) and an asynchronously updated slow channel (vision-language features), so the policy reacts to proprioception within a chunk while tolerating stale vision. Second, a latency-adaptive flow schedule treats in-flight actions as inpainting conditioning and emits actions in one denoising step per call, letting one trained model adapt to varying hardware latency. Requiring minimal modification to existing architectures, $π\mathbf{R}^2$ can be finetuned from a pretrained policy: applied to GR00T-N1.7 on a real xArm6+XHand platform, it replans closed-loop roughly $4\times$ faster than the base policy (~$25$Hz on an A5000 GPU), acting on a fresh observation every $40$ms. Across simulation and real-world manipulation tasks, $π\mathbf{R}^2$ improves the success rate by up to $23\%$ in simulation and $30\%$ in the real world over the strongest baseline. Project page: https://pi-r2-flow.github.io/

</details>


### [168] [INTACT: Isomorphic Intent-to-Action Learning for Search-Free World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26056)
*Junhan Sun,Hao Zhao,Guofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: INTACT是一个端到端的JEPA模型，将带动作标签的无奖励轨迹转化为可部署的意图到动作接口，无需昂贵的测试时搜索即可从期望的场景变化中恢复动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前向潜在世界模型虽然能预测动作如何改变场景，但需要通过昂贵的测试时搜索来为期望的变化恢复动作。需要一种能直接实现意图到动作转换的方法，避免搜索开销。

Method: 使用基于四槽语法的同构架构，通过相同的预测器在局部和全局运动意图之间建立动作规律语义。利用非对称端点梯度将物理后继和未来目标锚定，结合表示学习和控制，无需点对点潜在匹配或全局线性动态。

Result: 在四个官方LeWM任务上，单轮训练、零搜索模型分别达到85.78%、100.00%、97.67%和97.89%的成功率。使用384个候选序列（而非9000个）的局部CEM达到96.86%的宏观成功率，采样减少23.44倍，比纯CEM提高16.00个百分点。共享四任务编码器达到89.39%的E5 Direct宏观成功率。

Conclusion: INTACT提供了一种无需搜索的意图到动作转换方法，通过同构架构和动作规律语义实现了从RGB证据到动作有效潜在意图坐标的完整转换，同时支持直接推断（2.9-5.5毫秒）和可选验证采样。

Abstract: Forward latent world models predict how actions change a scene, but recover actions for a desired change only through expensive test-time search. We introduce INTACT (INtent-To-ACTion), an end-to-end JEPA that turns action-labeled, reward-free trajectories into a deployable intent-to-action interface. Each transition supplies physical intent $z_{t+1}-z_t$, while a future goal supplies deployment intent $\operatorname{sg}(z_g)-z_t$. The architecture is isomorphic between the local and goal motion-intent backbone-input graphs through an identical four-slot grammar and shared parameters, and between supported local and goal motion-intent families through action-law semantics induced by the same predictor rather than pointwise latent equality. INTACT also provides intact transfer from RGB evidence to action-effective latent intent coordinates and from intent families to their corresponding action-law families. Asymmetric endpoint gradients ground physical successors and fix future goals as anchors, joining representation learning and control without pointwise latent matching or globally linear dynamics. The resulting coordinates support a robust distributional action law: its conditional mean serves directly as a search-free policy, while sampling remains available for diversity or optional verification. On the four official LeWM tasks, one-epoch, zero-search models reach 85.78\%, 100.00\%, 97.67\%, and 97.89\% success. Optional local CEM centered on the Direct plan reaches 96.86\% macro success using 384 instead of 9,000 candidate sequences, reducing sampling by $23.44\times$ while improving pure CEM by 16.00 points. One shared four-task encoder reaches 89.39\% E5 Direct macro and improves every task over jointly trained LeWM, while predicted--expert action-family kNN tracks Direct success at $r=0.954$. Direct inference takes 2.9--5.5 ms.

</details>


### [169] [Shared Voxel-Map-Based Cooperative Indoor UAV Guidance with a Multi-Agent Soft Actor-Critic Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25728)
*Thomas Hickling,Dylan Wynne,Yu Su,Nabil Aouf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个结合共享体素地图世界模型与多智能体Soft Actor-Critic控制器的室内无人机协同导航框架，通过共享地图表示和去中心化控制实现高效协同导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多无人机在GNSS拒止室内环境中的协同导航问题，需要同时实现空间感知融合和去中心化控制，传统方法在复杂环境中表现有限。

Method: 采用共享体素地图世界模型，将多无人机360度LiDAR观测融合为统一占用地图，转换为紧凑的鸟瞰图表示，结合近场障碍物观测、目标信息和同伴状态，使用多智能体Soft Actor-Critic控制器进行集中训练、分散执行。

Result: 在模拟环境中达到90.3%的走廊导航成功率，优于A*规划、人工势场控制器和先前方法；通过离线模仿微调适应现实环境，在真实GNSS拒止室内环境中实现稳定双无人机协同操作。

Conclusion: 共享体素地图表示为学习型室内无人机协同导航提供了有效且可扩展的空间基础，结合集中训练分散执行框架，能够实现稳定高效的协同操作。

Abstract: This paper presents a cooperative indoor UAV guidance framework that combines a shared voxel-map world model with a multi-agent Soft Actor-Critic (MASAC) controller. Multiple drones fuse 360 LiDAR observations into a common world-frame occupancy map, which is converted into a compact bird's-eye-view (BEV) representation and provided to each agent as an ego-aligned local crop. This integrate-in-world, act-in- ego design enables consistent multi-UAV spatial fusion whilst retaining decentralised continuous control. The policy combines BEV map features, near-field obstacle observations, and compact goal and peer-state information within a centralised-training, decentralised-execution framework. In simulation, the learned controller achieves a 90.3% success rate in corridor navigation, outperforming Astar planning, an artificial potential field controller, and a prior guidance method. To address residual sim-to-real mismatch, the simulation-trained policy is further adapted using offline imitation fine-tuning from real-world data. Real-world experiments in GNSS-denied indoor environments demonstrate stable two-UAV cooperative operation across increasingly chal- lenging obstacle layouts. The results show that shared voxel-map representations provide an effective and scalable spatial substrate for learned cooperative indoor UAV guidance.

</details>
